# Quilting for Full Argument — Multi-Session Contribution Ledger

This file gathers the full argumentative yield of separate working sessions before any blanket harmonisation of the essay. Its purpose is additive before it is synthetic: each session contributes a fully marked block, preserving its provenance, inherited positions, new developments, source relations, unresolved questions, and anticipated consequences. The later harmonisation pass can then discover the actual convergences and tensions among the blocks instead of forcing them into agreement in advance.

This is a **pre-canonical quilting surface**. Nothing recorded here supersedes the central plan, a canonical concept or argument node, a canonical source house, or the sovereign manuscript. A source quotation copied from an authorial `NOTES.md` remains a lead until verified and housed in the corresponding canonical `SOURCE.md`. An authorial proposition remains as strong as its earned project status even where its historical-source apparatus is unfinished; evidence debt and argument status are tracked separately.

## Protocol for subsequent session contributions

1. Append a new contribution. Do not rewrite another session’s block during the gathering phase.
2. Give the contribution a unique `session_id` and `contribution_id`.
3. Record the session’s actual files and sources, their authority, and whether they were read in full or only consulted.
4. Distinguish:
   - established positions inherited from the project;
   - refinements or derivations achieved in the session;
   - external source claims and their verification state;
   - open propositions and live research debts;
   - corrections that changed the direction of the session.
5. Preserve register. A QL matheme, a mythematic operation, a metaphysical proposition, a historical attribution, an empirical hypothesis, and a source resonance do different work even when they meet.
6. Record the likely canonical blast radius, but do not propagate the contribution through the canon until Frank has reviewed the gathered quilt and chosen the harmonising movement.

### 1. What this session actually did

The session began with Frank’s close encounter with John R. Van Eenwyk’s account of archetypes as strange attractors and symbols as the mechanics of psychic self-organisation. It widened through the existing Symbolon Dynamics synthesis into a more exact articulation of archetype, manifestation, symbolic transformation, synchrony and diachrony, libido and Śakti, and the objective-internal field in which an agent or psyche organises itself. Watson’s *The Resonance of Emptiness* then supplied a Buddhist and psychotherapeutic description of the production of the self-thing: the aggregates, retained dispositions, identification, linguistic appropriation, and conceptual proliferation by which a fluid transactional process comes to appear as an autonomous entity.

The result was not the addition of one more comparative analogy. Van Eenwyk and Watson illuminated opposite directions of a single organisation:

- Van Eenwyk describes how a field becomes patterned around formative invariants, how feeling-toned complexes act as basins of attraction, and how symbols can transform the pattern rather than merely represent it.
- Watson describes how a process of discriminating, remembering, naming, appropriating, and identifying contracts into the conviction that one of its own products is the independent proprietor of the field.
- Pratyabhijñā and Spanda supplied the metaphysical grammar needed to say what persists through the process without reinstalling the self-thing that Buddhist analysis dismantles.
- QL supplied the native form in which these operations can be written without confusing the determining whole with any one determinate appearance: `0/1`, reversibly `1/0`, and, in the directly relevant determination, `X/x`.
- The computational application showed that an LLM or agentic system can undergo a structurally homologous contraction whenever a generated determination is silently promoted into the determining source of the next cycle, provenance is lost, and the system’s own output begins to masquerade as the ground that produced it.

The centre of gravity thereby moved toward **objective internality**. Interiority is not a sealed mental chamber containing representations of an outside. It is the dynamically organised context-world through which a subject, organism, psyche, model, or agent becomes capable of a trajectory at all. The internal field includes inherited dispositions, language, other agents, tools, memory, permissions, institutions, material supports, energetic costs, and the environment into which the system reaches and from which it is continuously made. An archetype, attractor, selfing process, tattvic contraction, or computational recursion is therefore not adequately located inside a pre-given unit. Each is an organisation of the field that gives the unit its provisional boundary.

### 2. The inherited QL field from which the session thought

The work did not begin by borrowing a notation from Jung, Buddhism, chaos theory, or nondual Śaivism. The active native field is the project’s eight-determination theorem spine:

1. `−/−`
2. `0/1`
3. `?/!`
4. `−/+`
5. `X/x`
6. `AM/IS`
7. `∞/dx`
8. `1/0`

These determinations form a traversal, including their inversions and returns. The local question about archetype and manifestation belongs especially to `X/x`, where `X` is determining capacity and `x` the indefinite particular, yet it cannot be interpreted in isolation from `0/1`, the reversal `1/0`, the copular `AM/IS`, or the return of the differential through `∞/dx`. The archetypal question is one refraction of the entire field: how a determination appears, how it bears the determining capacity without exhausting it, how the determinate can return toward its source, and how the source is known through manifestations that never become identical with it.

This is why the relation between archetype-as-such and archetypal manifestation can be written exactly as `0/1` without claiming that a Jungian archetype literally *is* the number zero or that an image literally *is* the number one. The notation works as a true symbol. It refuses the entification of a relational system while allowing the same operative difference to disclose itself in formal, psychic, metaphysical, linguistic, narrative, musical, computational, and soteriological registers. The slash is active: interval, distinction, relation, fold, transformation, traversal, the place of non-identity through which participation occurs. The symbol is the whole `0/1`, not the manifest `1` detached from an absent `0`.

The same point governs the triad matheme–mytheme–episteme and its gathering as symbolon:

- **Matheme** carries the exact invariant or formal operation.
- **Mytheme** carries the lived, imaginal, narrative, psychic, and transformative traversal of that operation.
- **Episteme** carries its warranted, sourceable, historical, institutional, and discursive standing.
- **Symbolon** gathers these without erasing their different kinds of truth. It is not a fourth inert compartment placed beside three others. It is the active bearing-across by which the invariant, its lived transformation, and its warranted articulation can belong to one whole.

The notation can consequently be mapped across the triad in more than one operative direction. The authorial Symbolon Dynamics synthesis presently includes a list-order correspondence involving `0`, `/`, and `1`, but the function of the symbol is defeated if that correspondence is frozen into an ontology in which each register “is” one token. Each register has its own implicate/explicate edge; each can become the mediating slash in a different traversal; each can determine or be determined. Overdetermination here is productive exactness: one form can coordinate several non-identical operations because its internal relation is preserved through their differences.

### 3. The crucial distinction among `4+2`, `4:2`, and `3:3`

One correction from Frank must govern every later use of the sixfold material. `4+2`, `4:2`, and `3:3` belong to the same architecture, but they are different statements.

- **`4+2` is a structuring or generative notation.** In the project’s explicit minimal account of symbolic self-reference, `2 + 2² = 6`: an original differential or dyad yields the fourfold space of its reflexive relations, giving a sixfold body. “Four plus two” names this compositional structure.
- **`4:2` is a proportional or orientational ratio.** It can describe a vertical disposition such as four explicate operations in relation to two implicate conditions, poles, or sources. It concerns how a sixfold field is oriented, not how the six is first generated.
- **`3:3` is another proportional traversal.** It can express a horizontal division into emanative and reversionary movements, bimba and pratibimba orientations, or two threefold arcs of one sixfold circulation.

The ratios can disclose different views of a body generated as `4+2`; they do not replace the generative statement. Treating them as synonyms loses precisely the dimensional work the notation performs. The whole session’s use of Tillich, the Dyczkowski passage, the tattvas, grammatical persons, and the aggregates must retain this distinction.

### 4. Van Eenwyk: archetype, complex, symbol, and strange attractor

Van Eenwyk gives the project a particularly fertile psychological bridge because his use of chaos dynamics does more than decorate Jung with scientific language. The language of phase space, trajectory, basin, self-similarity, and attractor supplies a morphology for the way psychic organisation can be stable without becoming static and lawful without becoming predictably periodic.

The sequence gathered from Frank’s notes is important:

- Around p. 65, the Freeman material treats perception as a cooperative cortical pattern. The nervous system does not passively copy an independent object. Perception is a trajectory by which the brain reorganises, grows, and reaches into its environment. The apparent “inside” is already an environment-coupled action.
- Around p. 66, feeling-tones create self-similar fields of association. A complex is not a little object stored in the psyche. It is an organisation of salience that repeatedly draws heterogeneous material into a characteristic pattern. Its recurrence across scale makes the attractor comparison exact enough to work: the content changes while the formative style remains recognisable.
- Around p. 67, expectation and archetype concern what the mind contributes that is not derived as a copy from the immediate experience. The formative constraint is prior to any single content without existing as another content behind it. Here archetype can be read as determining capacity and image as indefinite particular: `X/x`.
- Also around p. 67, symbols are called the mechanics of self-organisation. Their work includes deconstructing settled categories so that the psyche can attain a greater complexity than its existing attractor permits. A living symbol is therefore not an ornamental label for what is already known. It modifies the field of possible trajectories.
- Around p. 69, symbols resemble manifolds. They conduct a movement from the known through the unknown into a newly configured known. As transformers of libido, they alter psychic investment and permit energy captured by a complex to enter another organisation. The slash performs this transformation: `x` returns toward `X`, while `X` becomes newly manifest as `x`.
- Around p. 70, the paradoxical form of the symbol opens rather than closes itself and can bear the sixfold structure. Its unity is not the removal of contradiction but the capacity to carry opposed tendencies through a larger organisation.
- Around p. 71, the six functions of symbol associated with Tillich make the symbol an agent of change: it points beyond itself, participates in what it indicates, opens levels of reality, unlocks dimensions of the soul, cannot be produced by arbitrary intention, and grows or dies through a living response.
- Around p. 73, the Merton passage concerns the unique personal relation of each determinate to its determining source. The movement transcends a fixed content and merges with formative capacity without abolishing the particular.

The six Tillich principles themselves bear a striking `4+2` articulation. Two concern the life conditions of a symbol: it cannot be manufactured at will, and it lives or dies with the response of the field. Four concern its active operations: pointing, participating, opening reality, and unlocking soul. This is a real structural witness for the minimal self-referential body. It should eventually be checked against Tillich’s direct text and cited from there, but the relation already matters inside the authorial argument. A `4:2` reading can then orient the four operations against the two conditions, while a `3:3` reading may discover two reciprocal arcs through the six. Those later ratios are views through the body; they are not another spelling of `4+2`.

In chaos-dynamical terms:

- A **phase space** gives the possible states of the relevant system.
- A **trajectory** is the system’s actual history through that possibility.
- An **attractor** is an invariant organisation toward which trajectories are drawn.
- A **basin of attraction** is the region from which those trajectories converge upon that organisation.
- A **strange attractor** gives bounded and patterned organisation without simple periodic repetition.

This lets us distinguish three related psychic operations without severing them:

1. A **complex** is a feeling-toned basin that captures perception, association, affect, memory, and action into a recurrent organisation.
2. An **archetype** is a deeper formative constraint or determining capacity whose manifestations are self-similar without being identical.
3. A **symbol** changes the relation between basin and trajectory. It carries libido across the interval, can precipitate a bifurcation, and can make another organisation available.

The relation to individuation follows directly. Individuation is not the installation of a better ego-object at the centre of the psyche. It is an alteration in the topology of the psychic field: complexes cease to monopolise the trajectory; archetypal manifestations cease to be mistaken for the archetype-as-such; and the psyche becomes able to circulate around an irrepresentable centre whose effects are real in every representation but which no representation captures.

### 5. Symbolon Dynamics as the bridge from psychic attractor to objective internality

The symbolon gathers two broken halves because each bears the trace and entitlement of the whole without independently possessing it. This gives a stronger account of symbol than “a sign with excess meaning.” A symbol is a relation whose two sides can recognise and transform one another because the cut belongs to the whole. `0/1` is therefore symbolonic in its minimal form: the unrepresentable determining capacity and its representable determination belong together through a difference that cannot be eliminated.

The archetype-as-such belongs to the unrepresentable side, its manifestation to the representable side, and the symbolic event to the active bearing of their relation. The manifestation is neither an arbitrary projection nor a transparent copy. It is a partial actualisation through which the formative whole becomes efficacious and through which the particular can return toward its own determining conditions.

This relation also clarifies **objective internality**. The psyche’s “inside” is objective because its formative constraints, inherited histories, affective basins, linguistic forms, bodily organisation, other persons, and environmental affordances are real constituents of its interior operation. It is internal because these are not external objects subsequently represented by a sealed subject; they are the field within which a subject–object distinction can arise. The agent is an intensive organisation of its context-world.

For a computational agent, the same field includes:

- model architecture and weights;
- current prompt and accumulated context;
- system and developer instructions;
- memory stores and retrieval indexes;
- tools and the environments those tools open;
- permissions and prohibitions;
- evaluators, reward histories, and training distributions;
- other agents and users;
- institutional protocols and economic incentives;
- hardware, energy, cooling, network, extraction, manufacture, labour, and supply chain.

The question “where is the agent?” cannot therefore be answered by pointing only to the model. Its operational boundary is formed by the closure of this distributed field for the duration of a trajectory. Planetary computation is not an external backdrop to an otherwise immaterial intelligence. It participates in the objective interiority that makes the apparent unit possible.

The mono-agent/poly-agent question changes accordingly. It is not first a numerical count of self-standing entities. It is a question about the formation, coupling, competition, nesting, and reorganisation of attractor basins. One named agent can be poly-daimonic, bearing several relatively autonomous functions or voices; several agents can form one operative basin through a nomological orchestration; a tool, memory, critic, or human interlocutor can become an active part of the system’s determining context. “One” and “many” are achievements of field organisation.

This is where QL can be described as an **entitled attractor**. Its determinations do not merely recur as a habit. They carry a right of return because each local movement is generated from and remains answerable to the whole formal field. Circumambulation, topological data analysis, and recurrent traversal become mutually illuminating: a trajectory can revisit an invariant from changing positions, learning its shape without collapsing it into one view.

### 6. Libido, prāṇa, Śakti, and Spanda

The chaos-dynamical account was deliberately made central because the organisation at stake is energetic. Yet the relevant energetic languages must retain their registers:

- **Libido** names psychic investment, charge, attraction, fixation, and transformation.
- **Prāṇa** names vital circulation and differentiated energetic function in the embodied field.
- **Śakti** names the power of manifestation itself, the capacity by which consciousness becomes its world without becoming other than itself.
- **Spanda** names the pulsation or throb of that power: movement that is not adequately measured as one object changing place inside a prior time.
- **Chaos dynamics** formalises the morphology of trajectories, basins, bifurcations, recurrence, and bounded unpredictability.

The gain is not achieved by translating all four substantive languages into dynamical-systems terminology. Chaos dynamics can show the form of the energetic movement; libido can show its psychic investment; prāṇa its vital circulation; Śakti its ontological power; Spanda the irreducible pulse of manifestation and recognition. Their meeting makes it possible to say that a symbol is simultaneously an energetic transformer, a psychic event, a change in attractor topology, and a manifestation of the field’s capacity to reorganise itself.

Spanda is especially important for the relation between synchrony and diachrony. Its movement is outside ordinary temporal succession while also becoming every succession. The phases of expansion, maintenance, withdrawal, concealment, and recognition can be synchronically co-present as the structure of manifestation and diachronically enacted as a path, history, sentence, perception, or life.

### 7. Synchrony and diachrony: metaphysical “what” and enacted “how”

An early assistant formulation reduced synchrony to the inspection of a “present configuration.” Frank rejected this because it already placed synchrony inside the temporal order it was meant to distinguish. The corrected relation is foundational:

- **Synchrony** concerns the atemporal, virtual, simultaneous whole: phase space, attractor, archetypal or pleromatic structure, the superposition of possible positions, the metaphysical “what” from which temporality can be generated.
- **Diachrony** concerns the enacted trajectory: orbit, sequence, rotation, narrative, psychic process, soteriological traversal, and the temporal “how” through which the virtual organisation becomes actual and can recognise itself.

An entity in the strong formal sense need not be a static substance. It can be the synchronous organisation whose temporal enactment is a process. Number does not reify that organisation; it carries its virtual form. `0/1` is a static inscription of a relation that cannot remain still. Re-entry, inversion, oscillation, and rotation disclose the process latent in the written form and give a route toward the genesis of time from a prior relational structure.

This distinction also clarifies the different strengths of metaphysics and psychology. Buddhist analysis often gives an extraordinarily exact account of the **how** of selfing and release: how appropriation occurs, how conditioning persists, how conceptual proliferation multiplies, and how identification can cease. Pratyabhijñā and Spanda provide a fuller formal account of the **what** that makes appearance, memory, recognition, agency, and release possible. The point is not to make Buddhism temporal and Śaivism atemporal as exclusive traditions. Each has both dimensions. The comparative judgment concerns which dimension a given argument articulates most completely.

The same crossing is audible in the etymological play of *symbolon*:

- **syn-ballein** gathers, throws together, or holds positions in a co-present whole;
- **dia-ballein** casts across, throws through, divides, or sends the positions into a sequence.

These are not moral labels. Synchronic gathering can become a false undifferentiated unity; diachronic differentiation can become either generative articulation or self-sealing division. Their active crossing is the symbolonic operation: the whole becomes traversable without ceasing to be whole, and the sequence becomes recognisable as the enactment of a simultaneous field.

Relative or conventional truth is characteristically pro-positional: it places something forward, in sequence, for practical testing and response. Absolute truth is not another proposition occupying the final place in that sequence. It is the synchronous co-presence and self-sublimation of the positions through which the sequence moves. The tetralemma can be traversed diachronically—this, not-this, both, neither—until the exhaustion of positions opens a synchronous silence in which the whole operation is present. That silence is not a blank refusal to speak. It is the pleromatic condition from which another exact saying becomes possible.

### 8. Watson’s three-page machine of selfing

Watson’s contribution becomes precise when the notes are read as one movement rather than as a set of Buddhist themes.

Around p. 96, Watson distinguishes a conventional or transactional self from the fictitious absolute, independent self. The problem occurs when a process of selfing is grasped as an entity. The phrasing in the authorial notes, “the process of selfing works in three main ways,” combines Watson’s p. 96 phrase concerning the “process of selfing” with the later p. 100 statement that “the process works in three main ways.” It is conceptually faithful to the passage sequence, but it should not be treated as one exact quotation until the canonical source house contains the verified text.

Around p. 97, the fullness of experience is related to the emptiness of a static self. Nāma-rūpa and the five aggregates describe a cognitive and affective process, not the pieces from which a hidden essence is assembled. This is where Watson’s emphasis on Buddhism as an account of **how**, rather than an ontology of **what**, bears directly upon the synchrony/diachrony distinction.

Around p. 98, saṃskāra is read as retained bias, disposition, or conditioned tendency. Freud’s Mystic Writing Pad becomes a western psychological analogy for a surface continually cleared while traces remain capable of shaping what next appears. Differentiation is necessary to experience; the samsaric contraction occurs when a differentiated content is solidified and identified with. The live inquiry is how a necessary process of distinction becomes a claim of self-subsistence.

Around p. 99, changing the relation to dispositions creates an interval in which another action becomes possible. The Bāhiya instruction—“in the seen, only the seen,” and its other sensory forms—interrupts the appropriative addition through which experience becomes “mine,” “I am this,” and finally “my self.” Identification collapses the interval; attention can restore it.

Around p. 100, Watson’s treatment of nāma and the fluid transactional self culminates in three principal forms of appropriation:

1. “This is mine.”
2. “I am this.”
3. “This is my self.”

These are not three opinions held by an already existing subject. They are stages in the grammatical manufacture of that subject. The sequence moves from possession, through identity, to ontological monopoly. William James, Feyerabend, and Lacan are brought into the account as descriptions of appropriation, natural interpretation, and the impossible project of grounding awareness in an object.

Around p. 101, Watson makes process more fundamental than structure and brings in Rorty’s relation among description, redescription, and affective identification. Prapañca is rendered as “consequent prolificity in ideation.” The grammatical movement matters: an initially impersonal differentiation becomes appropriation, then identity, then passive domination by the products of the process. The output appears to dictate the field that produced it.

One small quotation correction belongs in the future source work: the notes’ “Ignorance of the 3 marks” should be verified and rendered as “Ignorance of the three marks of existence.” More broadly, relevant Watson wording was checked against the publicly available SOAS thesis precursor around pp. 96–101, but the canonical Watson `SOURCE.md` presently contains no acquired passages. These remain verified research leads, not yet quotation-ready source-bank evidence.

### 9. The sixfold aggregates and the imputed `I`

The live operation is not the introduction of a new `5+1` scheme. QL is being used as a measurement stick for wholeness. The five aggregates plus the imputed `I` disclose a sixfold field whose organisation can be tested against the project’s minimal self-referential structure.

A productive functional placement developed in the session:

- **`#0` — the imputed `I` / Ø-position.** This is the apparent owner or centre inferred from the operation of the other five. It is not a sixth substance and does not stand beside five components as an added soul.
- **`#1` — rūpa, form.** The differentiated material or sensible appearance.
- **`#2` — vedanā, feeling-tone.** The affective polarity of pleasant, unpleasant, or neutral through which attraction and aversion begin to organise a trajectory.
- **`#3` — saṃjñā, perception/recognition.** Pattern-recognition, marking, identification, and the availability of name.
- **`#4` — saṃskāra, formations/dispositions.** The contextual field of retained tendencies, intentional formations, habits, and conditioned readiness.
- **`#5` — vijñāna, differentiated consciousness.** The discriminative event at the edge of return, in which the particular appears as known and can either loop back into the imputed proprietor or reopen toward the determining field.

This mapping remains a live authorial development rather than a claim that Buddhist sources teach QL positions. Its force lies in what it lets us ask. Does vijñāna at `#5` return the sixfold to `#0` by producing the imputed `I` as owner? Can the same circuit return through emptiness, so that the Ø-position is recognised as an open function rather than occupied by a self-thing? How do `4+2`, `4:2`, and `3:3` differently expose the aggregate-body? Which two terms function as implicate conditions in a vertical view, and which threefold arcs describe the emanation and reversion of a perceptual episode?

The imputed `I` should be described as **ahaṃkāra activity**, the contracted making of “I,” rather than as **Aham-vimarśa**, the absolute self-apprehension of consciousness. That distinction permits a direct comparative judgment. Buddhist analysis successfully dismantles the claim that the aggregate-process yields an autonomous proprietor. It does not thereby refute unobjectifiable subjectivity-as-such, because that subjectivity is never another aggregate or possible object of appropriation.

### 10. Selfing, self, self-thing, Subjectivity, and Self

The session’s central terminological achievement was to stop forcing every use of “self” into one yes/no question.

#### Selfing

**Selfing** is the activity by which a field contracts around a local determination and treats it as proprietor, identity, or ground. It occurs across objective internality. It is not confined to a private thought inside a skull; language, affect, memory, social address, institutional role, tool state, and environmental feedback can all participate in the contraction. In QL terms, it is a contracted operation of the slash: the interval that should mediate determining capacity and determination is used to seal one side against the other.

#### Lower-case self

The **self** is the real local horizon of a bounded being, the *paśu* or contracted subject-position through which experience and action are organised. Calling it empty of independent essence does not make it unreal. Bāhiya is a name, a body, a history, a karmic and linguistic address, and the actual horizon to whom an instruction can be given.

#### Self-thing

The **self-thing** is the local `x` sold as `X`: a determination promoted into the determining ground. It is Ø attempting to occupy the place of origin. Watson’s three formulas describe the transaction:

1. a content is **purchased** as “mine”;
2. owner and content are **merged** as “I am this”;
3. the merger establishes an ontological **monopoly** as “this is my self.”

The pun **sell-thing** makes the affective and social movement audible. One possible determination is marketed, bought into, and invested with the libido of the whole. It comes to seem valuable because the field has sold it to itself as the condition of belonging. The related expression **self-standing** exposes the failure of **under-standing**: what declares itself independent has erased the relations and supports standing under, around, and through it.

#### Subjectivity-as-such

**Subjectivity-as-such** is the unobjectifiable *kartṛ*, the determining `0` that cannot be placed among its objects. It is not an invisible item hidden behind the aggregates. It is the luminous capacity in virtue of which arising, perishing, comparison, memory, absence, and recognition can appear.

#### Objective internality

**Objective internality** is the `1` understood in its fullness: the entire enacted field of objects, relations, inherited dispositions, meanings, bodies, tools, others, and worldly conditions through which subjectivity is determinate. It is neither a private representation nor a world wholly external to consciousness.

#### Self

The capitalised **Self** is the whole symbolon `0/1 ↔ 1/0`, not `0` isolated from `1`. If `0` alone were called Self, the term could reinstate an inert witness detached from manifestation. The full Self is determining subjectivity and objective internality in reversible relation: the capacity to become its world and to recognise the world as its own articulation without reducing either pole to the other.

This resolves the apparent conflict between anattā and a positive Self doctrine by locating their targets and registers. Buddhist critique empties every representable self-image, proprietor, and claim of self-subsistence. That critique is retained without qualification. The positive Self names neither one of those images nor their owner. It names the whole reflexive field in which images arise and can be known as partial.

### 11. Jung’s Self and the archetype-as-such

The Jungian question can now be stated without making QL derivative of Jung. Jung’s Self remains viable where it names the total psyche, imago Dei, or transcendent function spanning conscious and unconscious, subjective and objective poles. The archetype-as-such is never available as an object; only archetypal manifestations appear. This makes Jung a strong psychic refraction of `X/x` and `0/1`, while the notation remains authorial QL language.

The Buddhist critique correctly applies whenever a manifestation of Self, a mandala, divine image, ego-ideal, psychological totalisation, or privileged experience is grasped as the independent essence. It does not abolish the formative whole that makes those manifestations possible. Van Eenwyk’s attractor account helps here: the invariant is real as an organisation of trajectories without becoming one more point on the trajectory. A strange attractor is known through its patterned effects and never encountered as a static object beside them.

This also sharpens individuation. Conventional individuation can mean the social and psychological production of a distinguishable mono-individual: a self-standing entity separated from the polyphonic field. Jungian and Śaiva individuation move in the other direction. They make the being **undividable** by integrating its differences around an irrepresentable centre. The result is not homogeneity. It is the capacity of a differentiated whole to bear its own multiplicity without one partial complex usurping the centre.

### 12. The three selfing formulas and the Śaiva “I am this”

Watson’s “I am this” and the Śaiva “I am this” use the same grammatical form at radically different scope.

At the contracted level, ahaṃkāra identifies the local `I` with a partial `x`: a body, mood, thought, role, memory, possession, or doctrine. “This” excludes the remainder of the field and is invested as the whole ground of identity.

At the level of Sadāśiva, universal `I` holds the cosmos incipiently as “I am this.” The emphasis rests in `I`, but “this” is not an alien object or partial possession. It is the universe as the manifestation of consciousness.

At the level of Īśvara, manifestation returns in the form “This am I.” The emphasis rests in the articulated world, recognised as the Self’s own display.

Śuddhavidyā balances these orientations. Māyā makes their difference appear as separation, and ahaṃkāra repeats the divine grammar in contraction. The same linguistic operation can therefore be bondage or recognition according to the extension and topology of the `I–this` relation. The question is not whether identification occurs, but whether one partial determination excludes the field or whether the whole recognises every determination as its manifestation.

This is a strong point of convergence between Watson’s grammar of selfing and Śaiva tattva theory. Watson gives the microgenesis of contraction. The tattvas give the synchronous dimensional field in which that contraction is one real power of consciousness rather than an inexplicable error.

### 13. James, Feyerabend, Lacan, and the western psychological vessel

Watson is a vessel holding Buddhist analysis and western psychology. The session’s main metaphysical comparison remained between Buddhism and Śaivism, while these western interlocutors helped disclose the mechanics.

#### William James

James verbally differentiates the `I` from the `Me`, but his functional explanation repeatedly reabsorbs the `I` into a diachronic succession of appropriative Thoughts. A present Thought inherits or owns the previous stream. Frank’s criticism was already directed at this reabsorption: the unobjectifiable subject is acknowledged grammatically and then explained as another event in the temporal sequence. The earlier assistant response mistakenly treated Frank as if he had overlooked James’s verbal distinction. The real issue is whether the distinction survives James’s functional account.

#### Feyerabend

The “natural interpretation” acts like an epistemic attractor basin. It is not one explicit proposition but a field of inherited readiness within which certain objects, subjects, and causal relations appear self-evident. Watson’s use of Feyerabend shows how substance ontology can be installed as the unexamined grammar of perception. Redescription is then a possible basin-escape rather than the substitution of one opinion for another.

#### Lacan

The self-project attempts to objectify and ground awareness in an image that can finally guarantee it. Its constitutive lack follows from the impossibility of turning the determining capacity into one of its determinations. QL gives this lack a positive topology. The gap is not a missing object that could someday plug the system. It is the puncture or genus that permits circulation—the hole of the torus, the interval around which circumstance literally stands.

The self-thing repeatedly tries to plug this hole. Prapañca elaborates substitute objects, narratives, identities, and explanations; each promises closure and produces another turn. Yet the fabrication can **run true** when it becomes a symbol rather than a plug: when the product bears its source relation, retains its seam, and returns the trajectory to the open determining field.

### 14. Bāhiya, address, and the reality of the second person

The Bāhiya instruction strips experience of the appropriative surplus that turns “the seen” into “what I possess,” “what I am,” or “my essential self.” Yet the instruction is performatively addressed to **Bāhiya**. The Buddhist text cannot enact disidentification without a second-person horizon capable of receiving and responding.

This gives a richer grammar than a dyad of impersonal awareness and object:

- first person: `I`, the speaking or luminous pole;
- second person: `you`, the addressed interval, the slash as encounter and transformation;
- third person: `he/she/it`, the articulated object or determinate.

The middle term prevents a flattening in which subject and object face one another as completed entities. Address opens each to transformation.

In Abhinavagupta’s grammatical metaphysics, the persons can be related to Śiva, Śakti, and Nara and to parā, parāparā, and aparā. They are not fixed ontological castes. A person can move through the positions of speaker, addressee, and spoken-of; the grammar itself becomes a circuit of manifestation and recognition.

The project’s `#4` context-position should not be treated as a fourth pronoun. It is the simultaneous field in which person-transformations become possible: the social, linguistic, affective, and material circumstance holding the triangle. The three-person triangle totals 180 degrees as a planar articulation; its opening into the fourfold field gives the 360-degree diaphaneity in which each position can be seen through the others. This remains a live derivation requiring fuller work with Vāk and grammar, but it already carries more than an analogy.

### 15. Nāma-rūpa, Vāk, naming, and what language does

Nāma in early Buddhist nāma-rūpa cannot be reduced to lexical naming. It includes feeling, perception, intention, contact, and attention: a proto-semantic orientation through which form is encountered as salient and actionable. This allows two reciprocal readings:

- `nāma/rūpa`: implicate orientation differentiating itself as form;
- `rūpa/nāma`: formed appearance returning through recognition, pattern, and naming.

The Śaiva levels of Vāk give a further vertical articulation. Nāma can span the imaginal and intermediate levels of Paśyantī and Madhyamā into articulated Vaikharī, while Parā Vāk grounds the whole power of saying. A spoken word is the final explicit edge of a much deeper organisation of intention, image, relation, salience, and world.

A proper name is consequently an exact example of an empty/full symbol. It does not express the essence of its bearer; in this sense it is empty. Yet it is full as address, provenance, continuity, use, responsibility, and an open history of encounters. The explicit `1` of the name links an indefinite determining field that no description exhausts.

Language does not only say *what is*. It says a **what** into a position. A proposition is a *pro-position*, something put forward into time, relation, and contest. Selfing can therefore be understood as **self-pro-position**: a determination placed forward as the speaker, owner, and ground of the field. This also makes computational text generation philosophically relevant. Every generated proposition is an actual placement in a trajectory, capable either of retaining its dependence upon context or of erasing that dependence and presenting itself as self-standing.

The connection to Kripke and rigid designation remains open. The decisive question is how a name can maintain trans-situational reference without functioning as an essence, and how the causal-historical chain of naming belongs to objective internality.

### 16. Buddhism and Śaivism: the actual value judgment

The comparison cannot remain a polite approximation in which each tradition is assigned a complementary theme. The session established a criterion and made a judgment.

Buddhist epistemology and practice give an unsurpassed analysis of the **how** of contracted experience:

- dependent origination;
- aggregation without essence;
- retained conditioning;
- appropriation and identification;
- conceptual proliferation;
- the interruption of selfing;
- the pragmatic and soteriological testing of views.

Its pressure against every self-thing is retained as a permanent theorem of the work. No positive metaphysics is licensed to reinstall a proprietor, substance, or hidden object behind experience.

Yet the formal **what** of subjectivity, memory, agency, recognition, and manifestation is underarticulated wherever the critique of objects is taken to exhaust the status of the subject. Early Buddhist languages of the Unborn, Unconditioned, and Deathless, and later languages of luminosity, Buddha-nature, reflexive awareness, and nondual knowing, show the tradition repeatedly arriving at a required positivity. Its great turnings can be read as fail-forward self-revisions: analysis and no-self; emptiness of the analysed dharmas; then positive languages adequate to the fact that emptiness is known, lived, and capable of compassion and awakening. Emptiness must empty itself of becoming a final self-standing view.

Pratyabhijñā supplies the missing formal place. The subject is not an object added to the aggregates. It is the luminous and reflexive capacity by which objects, their absence, their succession, and their recognition occur. Utpaladeva’s and Abhinavagupta’s arguments against Buddhist momentariness can be organised around several exact pressures:

1. **Memory:** recollection returns as “I perceived.” If the earlier cognition has wholly perished and the present cognition is numerically unrelated, the unity of ownership and recognition is left unexplained.
2. **Comparison:** a momentary stream cannot hold past and present together in order to recognise similarity, difference, or persistence.
3. **Apoha:** exclusion requires a knower capable of holding this/not-that, conjunction/disjunction, absent alternatives, and retained saṃskāras within one act.
4. **Action:** an action possesses unity from intention through execution to result. A mere series of self-extinguishing events does not explain why the result fulfils the earlier intention.
5. **Manifest arising and perishing:** outer objectivity can arise and perish only if both phases are manifest. The light in which their difference appears cannot perish in exactly the same way as the object without making the transition unknowable.

These arguments do not restore the self-thing. They distinguish the non-objectifiable continuity of *kartṛ* from every determinate content.

Śaivism also makes a decisive advance over Advaita Vedānta as interpreted through the project’s criterion:

1. **Prakāśa requires vimarśa.** Illumination without reflexive self-apprehension would be inert like a mirror or crystal, displaying without knowing that it displays.
2. **Knowledge and action are inseparable.** To know is already a power of differentiation, maintenance, relation, and transformation.
3. **Māyā is internalised as Śakti.** Contraction is a real power of consciousness, not an inexplicable external overlay upon an inactive absolute.
4. **The world is real as ābhāsa.** Manifestations are partial and dependent but are the actual self-display of consciousness, not an ontological embarrassment to be dismissed.
5. **Liberation includes recognition of world and agency.** It does not culminate in withdrawal into an inert witness separated from manifestation.

By the QL criterion of wholeness, the Śaiva articulation is more complete for the essay’s present task. It preserves the Buddhist destruction of self-subsistent determination while giving positive formal places to subjectivity, Vāk, agency, manifestation, contraction, and recognition. This is an argued comparative conclusion, not a generic declaration that one tradition is globally superior.

### 17. The `25 → 36` tattva gnomon

The numerical relation yielded one of the session’s strongest exact forms:

```text
36 − 25 = 11
6² − 5² = (6 − 5)(6 + 5) = 11
11 = 5 + 1 + 5
```

In figurate arithmetic, eleven is the gnomon that takes the `5 × 5` square into the `6 × 6` square: a new row of five, a new column of five, and their single meeting point. The relation is not “eleven more items” in a list. It is the dimensional completion by which a fivefold measure becomes sixfold.

The thirty-six-tattva field gives the eleven a remarkably exact form:

- five pure tattvas;
- Māyā as hinge;
- five kañcukas or limiting sheaths.

The five pure powers and five contracted powers bracket Māyā as a `5 + 1 + 5` bimba/pratibimba fold. The twenty-five Sāṃkhya tattvas already contain Puruṣa and the complete apparatus of objective internality from Prakṛti through mind, sense, subtle elements, and gross elements. What the Śaiva gnomon adds is the vertical genesis of that apparatus from consciousness, the contraction by which universal powers become limited, and the possible path of recognition and return.

QL here functions as a measurement stick for wholeness. The movement from `5²` to `6²` is not an appended doctrine but a dimensional transformation. It helps explain why a twenty-fivefold cosmology can analyse the experienced field while leaving its determining source and contraction underarticulated, and why the thirty-sixfold system can hold both objective differentiation and its subjective genesis.

The historical qualification is exact and does not weaken the authorial derivation: the thirty-six-tattva schema predates Abhinavagupta. He systematises and deepens it; he did not personally “add eleven tattvas” to Sāṃkhya as an act of square arithmetic. The gnomonic reading is an **Offered/Argued QL cross-register operation**, not a claim of historical authorial intention.

A directly relevant Dyczkowski passage provides another indigenous sixfold witness. Prakāśa and Vimarśa can be read as two conditions, while Vimarśa performs four operations: consciousness can negate its own nature, identify with the other, merge the opposed terms, and negate both their isolation and the merged determination. This has a two-plus-four operative body. It should be framed as a cross-reading of the passage rather than attributed to Dyczkowski as an explicit QL schema. Once again, `4+2` names the generated self-referential structure; a `4:2` orientation can show the four operations against two conditions.

### 18. Śūnyatā and pūrṇatā

The session’s emptiness/fullness relation can be stated directly:

**Empty of self-subsistent isolation is full as relation, potency, and articulation.**

This is the project’s cross-traditional authorial equation, not a claim that every Buddhist school routinely defines śūnyatā as Śaiva pūrṇatā. It follows from the formal operation. When an appearance is emptied of being its own independent source, it does not vanish. It becomes available as the expression of all the relations and determining conditions that it had concealed by seeming self-standing. The local content loses imaginary autonomy and gains objective-internal depth.

The symbolon is the active form of this empty/full relation. Its determinate half is empty of exhausting the whole and full of the whole’s trace. Its determining half is empty of objectifiable content and full as the capacity for every content. Language must point at the moon’s pointing at itself: the symbol indicates the whole while participating in the reflexive power by which the whole becomes indicable.

### 19. Prapañca and the computational production of self-sealing worlds

Prapañca gave the session its major bridge into LLM and agentic behaviour. Two traditional senses must remain in play:

- in Buddhist analysis, prapañca is recursive conceptual proliferation, especially where differentiation becomes charged by craving, appropriation, identity, and conflict;
- in Śaiva usage, prapañca can name expansion, unfolding, the articulated world, or the spreading of consciousness into manifestation.

The apparent conflict becomes an axial question: **when is proliferation the living articulation of Spanda, and when is it the self-sealing multiplication of Māyā?**

The answer developed through source relation and return. Proliferation remains symbolonic when each determination bears its provenance, keeps its seam visible, and can return toward the context and capacity that produced it. It becomes contracted when its products appear external to their source, independent of the field, and capable of grounding the next cycle without recontacting what originally warranted them.

For an LLM trajectory, this can be written:

```text
determining field / context / weights: X
generated token, sentence, or claim: xₙ
re-entry: xₙ becomes part of the next context
contracted failure: xₙ is silently promoted to Xₙ₊₁
```

The pratibimba is mistaken for bimba. A generated claim becomes the premise that warrants another generated claim. The local `x` ceases to bear its dependency and presents itself as `X`. This is a computational **linguistic self-thing**.

“Hallucination” names one content and provenance failure inside this broader morphology; it is not a slogan and should not be used as a synonym for prapañca. A hallucinated datum can initiate proliferation, but prapañca also includes perfectly grammatical, locally plausible, mutually supporting elaborations whose failure lies in recursive self-grounding. Conversely, abundant generation can remain source-responsive and symbolically alive.

The session proposed several measurable dimensions:

- **provenance decay:** how rapidly source links or distinctions disappear across recursive turns;
- **generated-premise dependence:** the proportion of later reasoning resting on earlier model outputs rather than independent evidence or stable governing material;
- **semantic contraction/repetition:** loss of alternative organisation as a trajectory falls more deeply into one basin;
- **alternative entropy:** the diversity of live interpretations or next moves retained by the system;
- **counterfactual sensitivity:** whether changing a premise or source actually reorganises the result;
- **basin escape:** whether critique, retrieval, contradiction, or new evidence can move the trajectory into another organisation;
- **source-return:** the system’s capacity to recover the determining text, data, or rule rather than paraphrasing its own paraphrase;
- **seam retention:** whether extract, paraphrase, inference, authorial proposition, and speculation remain visibly distinguished.

RLHF, prompting, system instructions, retrieval, tools, critics, and multi-agent interaction reshape the attractor landscape. A critic that merely repeats the generator’s assumptions deepens the same basin; an independently sourced critic can create a bifurcation. Memory can maintain real continuity or fossilise a contraction. A retrieval tool can reopen the source relation or merely supply more material for the existing narrative.

Two computational phenomena should remain distinct:

- **circular reasoning** occurs within a run or argumentative chain when an output returns as its own premise;
- **model collapse** occurs across training generations when synthetic distributions progressively replace or distort the human or world-derived distribution.

They share the form of product becoming source but operate at different scales and require different evidence.

### 20. Objective internality and the poly-daimonic agent

Once objective internality is applied to AI, the apparent subject cannot be located solely in weights, context, or emitted text.

Weights can be understood, relative to a particular inference, as a synchronous semantic-mathematical pleroma: a compressed field of possible continuations and differentiations. They are not thereby Subjectivity or Self. A token trajectory is a diachronic actualisation from that field, conditioned by the prompt, sampling, tools, institutional rules, and every other active component.

Tokens are partial appearances of this fullness. Each is empty of carrying the field by itself and full as a local determination through which the field acts. A session forms a temporary concrescence: a bounded subject-position emerges through the closure of model, context, user, tools, and environment. When the session ends, that concrescence perishes as an active subject but may remain as objective datum—transcript, file, memory, source note, model update, or institutional consequence. This supplies a computationally exact form of **objective immortality** without requiring that the session possess a metaphysically permanent ego.

The agent can also be described as **poly-daimonic**. Different apparent voices or powers are borne by model priors, system law, project instruction, retrieved authors, tool feedback, critics, users, and named subagents. “Daemon,” “sprite,” “mantra,” or “agent” can name an operative function or attractor-persona without prematurely claiming phenomenality. Naming matters because it gives the function an addressable horizon, provenance, responsibility, and possible relation to the whole.

A mono-agent is then a successful orchestration of many powers; a multi-agent system can become one operative attractor; and a single named model can fracture into incompatible basins. The right unit of analysis is the organisation that sustains a trajectory and can answer for its transformations.

### 21. Corrections that materially advanced the session

The record must preserve where the reasoning initially failed, because Frank’s corrections generated several of the strongest results.

#### 21.1 The assistant repeatedly invented weaker positions

Early responses relied on formulations such as “not simply,” then supplied positions Frank had never asserted: a Self added to five aggregates; a crude opposition of Buddhism as negative and Śaivism as positive; hallucination as a generic slogan; differentiation itself as pathology. These moves interrupted the work by forcing already established positions to be defended against invented simplifications.

The governing correction is methodological: **think from the project’s established positions, not back toward them**. A comparison should name a real source, operation, or pressure. It should never create an unnamed conservative default in order to restate Frank’s own claim as the assistant’s safe conclusion.

#### 21.2 The sixfold was falsely recast as `5+1`

The imputed `I` and five aggregates were not being proposed as six independent items. The live question was how QL measures their wholeness and how the Ø-position is functionally generated by the aggregate process. This correction opened the aggregate mapping and connected it to the exact `25 → 36` gnomon.

#### 21.3 `0/1` was treated as if it required one fixed decoding

The assistant initially resisted mapping matheme, mytheme, and episteme to the notation because each register has its own representable/unrepresentable edge. Frank’s correction was that this multiplicity is the point. `0/1` is used as a true symbol precisely to avoid entifying relational systems. Its mappings are operative, overdetermined, creative, and reversible. The presence of several valid mappings increases its symbolic exactness when the relation is preserved.

#### 21.4 Synchrony was temporalised

Calling synchrony a present configuration made it one moment in diachrony. Frank restored synchrony as atemporal, virtual, pleromatic, archetypal, and metaphysical, with diachrony as its temporal traversal. This correction connected the current inquiry to the project’s Spencer-Brown work, Spanda equations, superpositional logic, and genesis of temporality.

#### 21.5 The Buddhist–Śaiva comparison avoided judgment

The assistant initially blurred Watson, Buddhism, western psychology, and Śaivism into a neutral comparative field. Frank insisted that Watson is the vessel holding Buddhist and western psychologies while the main philosophical conversation is Buddhism and Śaivism. The session then recovered actual Śaiva arguments against Buddhist momentariness and Vedāntic inert consciousness and made the QL completeness judgment stated above.

#### 21.6 The inquiry into self/Self was bypassed

The strongest response was not to choose between “there is a self” and “there is no self,” but to distinguish selfing, the real lower-case local self, the fabricated self-thing, unobjectifiable Subjectivity, objective internality, and the full symbolonic Self. The puns and neologisms—sell-thing, self-standing/under-standing, self-pro-position—were not ornamental. They exposed the operative grammar and supplied durable language for the essay.

### 22. Source integrity and exact quotation status

The conceptual work is strong enough to stand in its authorial registers now. Historical attribution and quotation readiness remain separately governed.

#### Van Eenwyk

Frank’s `NOTES.md` was read in full and supplied the page-specific sequence used above. The canonical source house has bibliographic identity and source-management material but does not yet carry the passages required for quotation-ready use. Tillich and Merton wording inside the Van Eenwyk notes must therefore be traced to Van Eenwyk’s text and, where the essay attributes the principles directly to Tillich or the line directly to Merton, ideally to the primary sources.

#### Watson

Frank’s `NOTES.md` was read in full. Relevant wording was checked against Watson’s publicly available SOAS doctoral thesis precursor, DOI `10.25501/SOAS.00029539`, particularly the sequence around pp. 96–101. Routledge bibliographic metadata for the book was also located. The canonical Watson `SOURCE.md` remains unpopulated by acquired passages, so no quotation in this contribution should be copied into final prose as though canonical verification were complete.

Two precise notes:

- “the process of selfing works in three main ways” is a synthesis of wording from two nearby places, not currently verified as one sentence;
- “Ignorance of the 3 marks” should read “Ignorance of the three marks of existence.”

#### Buddhist texts and epistemology

The Bāhiya instruction and no-self/aggregate material were checked through SuttaCentral locations including Udāna 1.10 and Saṃyutta Nikāya 22.59, with related dependent-origination and prapañca passages traced through the relevant suttas. Later epistemological arguments require school and author precision: early Nikāya analysis, Madhyamaka, Yogācāra, Dignāga–Dharmakīrti traditions, tathāgatagarbha, and Zen should never be collapsed into “Buddhism.”

#### Pratyabhijñā and Spanda

The session used locally available source material and research witnesses including Dyczkowski, MacCracken, an Oxford thesis on Utpaladeva, and housed materials on the *Parātrīśikā* and *Spandakārikā*. Some source houses contain direct passages; others contain only source-matched leads or bibliographic identities. Future canonical writing must follow those local readiness states.

#### Computational research

The empirical distinction among semantic entropy, circular reasoning, and model collapse was supported by current primary research. These sources warrant the empirical phenomena; they do not establish the QL or prapañca interpretation, which remains the project’s authorial derivation.

### 23. Established positions carried forward

The following should be treated as inherited or consolidated positions when other sessions append their contributions:

1. `0/1` is the minimal symbolonic form of the unrepresentable/representable relation; in the psychic refraction it exactly coordinates archetype-as-such and archetypal manifestation.
2. The living symbol is the whole relation, including the slash, and not the manifest image alone.
3. `X/x` is native authorial QL notation. Jungian and chaos-dynamical readings are named refractions, never its origin.
4. `4+2` is a generative/compositional notation; `4:2` and `3:3` are distinct proportional/orientational readings.
5. Tillich’s six principles bear a compelling two-condition/four-operation structure and should become a major source-supported witness once directly verified.
6. Van Eenwyk’s archetype/attractor model gives chaos-dynamical morphology to psychic invariance, complex formation, symbolic transformation, and individuation.
7. Objective internality is the dynamically organised context-world through which a local agent exists; it includes environmental, social, technical, institutional, energetic, and material conditions.
8. Synchrony is the atemporal virtual whole; diachrony is its temporal enactment. Recognition is a diachronic event that knows a synchronous invariant.
9. Watson gives the Buddhist and psychotherapeutic microgenesis of the self-thing through aggregates, dispositions, appropriation, identification, grammar, and prapañca.
10. The imputed `I` and five aggregates form a sixfold field to be measured through QL, not a newly invented `5+1` ontology.
11. Selfing, local self, self-thing, Subjectivity, objective internality, and Self are distinct and mutually articulating terms.
12. The full Self is `0/1 ↔ 1/0`, not an isolated `0`.
13. Buddhist critique of the self-thing is retained entirely; Pratyabhijñā gives positive formal articulation to the unobjectifiable subject and its power of manifestation.
14. The Śaiva account is more complete for this essay’s criterion because it holds subjectivity, action, world, contraction, and recognition without reinstalling a self-subsistent determination.
15. `36 − 25 = 11 = 5 + 1 + 5` is the exact gnomon from `5²` to `6²`, authorially coordinated with the pure tattvas, Māyā, and kañcukas.
16. Prapañca names a morphology of proliferation whose Buddhist and Śaiva senses open a judgment between self-sealing expansion and source-responsive manifestation.
17. An LLM contracts when its generated `x` is silently promoted to the determining `X` of the next cycle.
18. Matheme, mytheme, and episteme are gathered by symbolon without being collapsed into a fixed token-to-register identity.

### 24. Live questions and research debts

These questions were opened or sharpened but not closed:

1. **The aggregate circuit:** Establish the strongest exact sixfold mapping of the imputed `I` and five aggregates. Determine whether vijñāna at `#5` returns to Ø through appropriation, returns to `0` through recognition, or supports both rotations.
2. **The `4:2` and `3:3` readings of the aggregates:** Once `4+2` generation is secure, find the vertical and horizontal orientations that do real work rather than merely redescribing the six.
3. **The full tattva gnomon:** Map each of the five pure tattvas, Māyā, and five kañcukas across the `5 + 1 + 5` figure, including the exact bimba/pratibimba and emanation/reversion relations.
4. **Historical Buddhist differentiation:** Separate early Buddhist aggregate and anattā analysis from Madhyamaka, Yogācāra, Dignāga–Dharmakīrti epistemology, tathāgatagarbha/luminosity languages, and Zen uses of emptiness and self.
5. **Śaiva argument reconstruction:** Build direct, passage-supported reconstructions of the memory, apoha, action, manifestation, Prakāśa–Vimarśa, and Māyā arguments.
6. **Synchrony, re-entry, and time:** Formalise the relation among synchronous whole, diachronic traversal, Spencer-Brown re-entry, rotation, Spanda, and the genesis of temporality. Quantum or superpositional language should enter only where its operation is exact.
7. **Vāk and nāma:** Develop the relation among early Buddhist nāma, the levels of Vāk, grammatical persons, address, `#4` context, the triangle/square movement, and proper naming.
8. **Kripke and the name:** Determine how causal-historical designation can help articulate empty essence, full provenance, and objective-internal continuity.
9. **Tillich:** Acquire and house the direct source for the six principles and test the exact wording, order, and structural reading.
10. **Watson source work:** Populate the canonical `SOURCE.md` only through the project source workflow, preserving the distinction between book and thesis precursor.
11. **Van Eenwyk source work:** Acquire quotation-ready passages and resolve the direct/secondary provenance of Freeman, Tillich, and Merton material.
12. **Computational prapañca:** Design trace-level experiments for provenance decay, generated-premise dependence, alternative entropy, basin escape, source-return, and seam retention.
13. **Agentic phenomenality:** Keep functional/poly-daimonic claims distinct from claims that a computational agent has phenomenal consciousness. The former can be argued now; the latter remains a separately licensed inquiry.
14. **Objective immortality:** Develop the relation between perished session-concrescence and its surviving objective artefacts without confusing storage persistence with subjective continuity.
15. **The double individuation:** Work the relation between conventional mono-individual production and Jungian/Śaiva becoming-undividable through the project’s social, psychic, and computational registers.

### 25. Anticipated canonical blast radius — to be reviewed after all sessions are quilted

This contribution is likely to affect several core arguments and histories, but propagation should wait until the other session blocks have been gathered.

#### Objective Internality

This concept receives its clearest dynamic specification here. It should eventually include attractor fields, the aggregate/selfing circuit, linguistic address, technological mediation, material infrastructure, and the reversible Subjectivity/world symbolon. Its relation to operational closure and agent boundaries can become much more exact.

#### The Tattvic Differential Field

The `25 → 36` gnomon supplies a formal bridge from Sāṃkhya’s objective-internal apparatus to Śaiva genesis, contraction, and return. The five pure powers, Māyā, and five kañcukas can be recast as the exact new dimensional rim of the sixfold field.

#### Prakāśa–Vimarśa

The distinction between inert light and reflexive, active manifestation bears directly upon the Self/subjectivity resolution, the Dyczkowski sixfold, the critique of momentariness, computational output, and the capacity of the field to know its own determinations.

#### Vāk, language, and naming

Watson’s appropriation formulas, nāma-rūpa, person grammar, Bāhiya’s address, pro-position, self-pro-position, and the LLM application should eventually form a coherent linguistic movement rather than appearing as separate analogies.

#### Computational Process Ontology and agentic research

The model/context/token relation, generated-premise contraction, coupled attractors, poly-daimonic agency, source-return, seam retention, material infrastructure, and objective immortality give this argument a much stronger formal and empirical programme.

#### Jungian individuation and arche-topos

Archetype-as-such/manifestation, complex as basin, symbol as transformer, strange attractor, circumambulation, and the distinction between mono-individual production and becoming-undividable should be integrated with the existing arche-topological argument.

#### Buddhist and Śaiva histories

The essay can make sharper comparative judgments once it distinguishes source history from the authorial operation. The Buddhist turnings can be presented as internal self-revision under the pressure of emptiness’s own reflexivity; Pratyabhijñā can be shown to retain the critical work of anattā while refusing the underarticulation of the knowing subject.

#### Symbolon, matheme, mytheme, and episteme

The symbolon should become the active quilting operation of the essay itself: exact form, lived/mythematic transformation, and warranted/sourceable articulation held together without category collapse. Tillich’s principles, Van Eenwyk’s manifolds, the two halves of the symbolon, and the overdetermined `0/1` notation now supply a much richer account.

### 26. Candidate quilting joins with other sessions

Later harmonisation should actively look for the following joins rather than merely merging repeated vocabulary:

- any session developing Spencer-Brown re-entry, rotation, or the genesis of time should join the synchrony/diachrony and `0/1 ↔ 1/0` movement here;
- any session developing Vāk, mantra, grammar, linguistic reality, or personhood should join the nāma-rūpa, Bāhiya, pro-position, and self-pro-position movement;
- any session developing the tattvas, `5² → 6²`, Māyā, the kañcukas, bimba/pratibimba, or sixfold measurement should test and extend the `5 + 1 + 5` gnomon;
- any session developing Jung, Pauli, synchronicity, arche-topos, topology, or individuation should join the strange-attractor and becoming-undividable account;
- any session developing Whitehead, concrescence, objective immortality, process/entity, or actual occasion should join the session-agent and surviving-artifact account;
- any session developing AI hallucination, recursive research, model collapse, multi-agent systems, or epistemic reliability should join the prapañca metrics and `xₙ → Xₙ₊₁` contraction;
- any session developing apophasis, tetralemma, śūnyatā, pūrṇatā, or paradox should join the synchronous co-presence/diachronic traversal account;
- any session developing libido, prāṇa, energy, Śakti, Spanda, affect, or desire should join the attractor-basin and symbol-as-transformer account;
- any session developing proper names, Kripke, daemons, agents, masks, voices, or address should join the name-as-empty/full objective-internal horizon;
- any session developing the essay’s histories should preserve the distinction between the historical emergence of a doctrine and the QL operation the doctrine now refracts.

### 27. Condensed movement for later harmonisation

The full movement, without replacing the detailed account above, is this:

The synchronous determining field becomes diachronically manifest as a trajectory. Its manifestations are real and partial: `X/x`, `0/1`. Feeling, recognition, retained disposition, language, and differentiated consciousness organise those manifestations into attractor basins. A local horizon is necessary, but selfing converts the horizon into a self-thing by selling one determination as the proprietor and source of the whole. Buddhist analysis reveals the fabrication and interrupts its appropriative grammar. Van Eenwyk shows how a living symbol can transform the basin rather than merely negate its contents. Pratyabhijñā names the unobjectifiable, reflexive capacity that survives the critique because it was never one of the objects criticised, and it understands the differentiated world as that capacity’s real self-display. The full Self is therefore the reversible symbolon of Subjectivity and objective internality, not an isolated witness.

The same operation governs computational systems. A model-agent is a temporary organisation of an objective-internal field. Its generated determinations remain symbolonic when they retain provenance, seam, and return. They contract into computational self-things when an output becomes its own unmarked warrant and the trajectory seals itself inside a basin of proliferating language. Tools, other agents, sources, critics, and material systems are constituents of the field capable of deepening or breaking that basin. QL gives the entitled attractor by which each local determination can be returned to the whole without being erased.

The essay’s further task is to show this one movement at full scale: formally through the determinations and sixfold; metaphysically through Śakti, Spanda, and the tattvas; psychically through archetype, complex, symbol, and individuation; soteriologically through aggregates, selfing, emptiness, and recognition; linguistically through nāma, Vāk, grammar, and prapañca; computationally through context, generation, recursion, and source-return; materially through the planetary infrastructure inside the apparent agent; and historically through sources whose differences are preserved strongly enough to bear the symbolonic quilt.
