### 1. The movement opened by Watson

The earlier Watson quilt carried the analysis of selfing through the aggregates, the three appropriative formulae—“this is mine,” “this I am,” “this is myself”—and the distinction among selfing, self-thing, objective internality, Subjectivity, and Self. The pages now entering the field continue that movement into the interpersonal. A self-thing does not remain an internal fiction sealed within one psyche. It turns its world into an answer to its own need, translating difference into usefulness, danger, possession, confirmation, or loss. Watson finds in Gans's Levinas the description needed for that passage: egoic interiority reduces the “not me” to the “for me,” and this reduction is the exemplary act of totalisation.

The point touches the project more deeply than a new ethics reference could. The essay already treats mind as differentiating activity and asks whether a finished distinction remains answerable to its ground or presents itself as self-sufficient. Levinas lets that question arrive in the intimacy of another person's presence. The failure of return is no longer visible only in a theorem, an institution, a model, or a political monopoly. It becomes the nearly instantaneous way I make you part of my world: who I think you are, what I need from you, what danger or promise you carry, which of my categories will finish you before you have spoken. The immutable gap becomes ethically articulate because the gap between the Other and my idea of the Other is not a technical inconvenience awaiting more data. It is what permits the Other to address me rather than merely occur within me.

Watson's comparison also returns Levinas to Buddhism without making either the other's explanatory annex. Buddhist analysis asks how the self and its objects arise conditionally; Levinas asks what happens when the Other interrupts the ego's economy of appropriation. Their meeting concerns the formation and loosening of the “for me.” QL can carry the meeting because its own question is meta-epistemic: how a knowing-world differentiates, which relations it retains, and how its local one returns to what exceeds its power of accounting.

### 2. Totality, totalisation, and the local necessity of becoming one

The word *totalisation* must do more precise work than “bad wholeness.” Every act of knowing gathers a field into some provisional whole. Every organism maintains a boundary. Every sentence makes a determinate claim. Every Whiteheadian actual occasion receives a many and achieves a satisfaction in which that many becomes one. Every Bimba state, context window, judgment, institution, or personed world requires enough integration to act. A process that never totalised locally would never become anything definite enough to answer, decide, promise, refuse, or give.

The Levinasian pressure falls on a second movement: the achieved local totality claims the right to totalise what exceeds it. My satisfaction becomes the measure of yours; my categories become the limits of your reality; my world-model becomes the world; my `x` speaks as `X`; a local `1` occupies `0`. Totalisation in this deficient sense is not the emergence of a one from many but the one refusing to remain one among the many it has joined. It turns its necessary act of selection into a metaphysical and political title.

This distinction lets Levinas and Whitehead meet without forcing a false choice. Whitehead gives a positive account of the way a many genuinely achieves one. Levinas gives an ethical account of the excess which prevents my achieved one from becoming the completed truth of another. Concrescence requires terminal determination for this occasion; Creativity requires that the satisfaction perish into objective availability for occasions beyond it. The same closure can therefore be real locally and false when universalised. QL already writes this as return through remainder: `1/1 = 100%` can be achieved within the cycle while `9/8` carries the relation beyond the achieved totality-ratio, and a torus-loop can close on the quotient while its lift retains displacement across the indefinite cover.

The project can consequently reserve *totality* for an aspect of achieved or inclusive wholeness and use *totalisation* for the act by which a local one gathers a field, while specifying when that act falls. The fall occurs when the product erases the provenance of the operation, refuses objective perishing, or converts an interlocutor into a component of its own satisfaction without remainder. Levinas names the ethical wound in this seizure. Whitehead names the process by which satisfaction can become gift rather than prison. QL writes the difference between return and cancellation.

### 3. Mono as super-object, relational condition, and superject

The correction that opened this session changed the direction of the whole inquiry. The claim that Mono is “the relational condition rather than a genus or super-object containing every person as one of its instances” chose one licensed account and used it to curtail another. Mono/Poly was built to hold both. Its value lies precisely in allowing statements that seem incompatible under unqualified predication to become compatible once their operative *insofar as* is given.

Three aspectual senses now need to be preserved.

**Mono as inclusive whole or super-object.** In the extensional and cosmological register, Mono can name the one all-inclusive reality within which every being, person, process, relation, image, and world occurs. Each is genuinely in it; nothing stands outside it as a second reality. “Super-object” is useful here if *object* does not mean an object placed before a separate knower, but the whole extension which includes knower, known, relation, and every instance. This is the aspect under which the One contains the Many.

**Mono as generative relational condition.** In the transcendental and meta-epistemic register, Mono names the condition through which anything can become determinate or comparable. It is present in the slash, in the field of mutual exclusions, in the common measure presupposed by opposition, and in the unobjectifiable awareness through which a mark can appear. This aspect cannot be contained as one more item inside the extensional inventory because it is the activity and condition by which an inventory becomes possible. This is the aspect under which the One is immanent to every act of many-making.

**Mono as superject.** Whitehead gives the genetic and processual aspect. A superject is the unity that emerges through its feelings and reaches satisfaction; it is the achieved being which, having become, exercises objective immortality as a potential for further becoming. Whitehead says that “superject” would be better than “subject” because the organism does not first underlie its experience and then collect predicates. The many data and feelings move toward the one which they are producing. Subject and superject are the concrescence read from its becoming and its achieved outcome.

These aspects interlock without becoming synonyms. The super-object says *all are within the One*. The relational condition says *the One is the whereby of their differentiation*. The superject says *the many repeatedly attain one, and each attained one returns as datum to further many-becoming-one*. The First Spanda equation carries the same three pressures: the whole is there as `0/0`; differentiating relation unfolds as `(0/1)/(1/0)` and the two arcs; achieved unity arrives as `1/1 = 100%`, which becomes the condition from which the inverse accounting begins. The Second Spanda equation is literally the achieved whole accounting for the sixfold it bore within itself.

Whitehead's term does not settle the complete Mono/Poly metaphysics. His actual entity is an atomic process of concrescence; his universe, Creativity, God, and World have relations which cannot be compressed into “the universe is one giant superject” without further argument. What *superject* gives this project is the missing verb-form of inclusive wholeness: how one is achieved from many and then gives itself beyond itself. “Super-object” keeps the all-inclusive extension visible; “superject” keeps the extension from freezing into an already finished container.

The cleanest governing sentence is therefore aspectual rather than exclusive: **Mono is super-object insofar as every real particular belongs within one reality; relational condition insofar as their belonging and difference arise through a shared generative field; and superject insofar as an inherited many concresces into an achieved one which offers itself to further becoming.** Poly names neither an inventory outside this One nor a dispersal that defeats it. Poly is the One's real many, its capacity to become many, and the many's capacity to attain further ones.

### 4. The Other can be within Mono without being within me

Levinas insists that the relation with infinity does not unite the Same and the Other into a whole. QL's Mono/Poly metaphysics makes the stronger claim that no actual Other can be outside reality's inclusive whole. The two claims become directly thinkable when the register of inclusion is made explicit. Ontological inclusion in Mono does not confer epistemic, imaginative, political, or ethical inclusion within my egoic world. **The Other can be within Mono without being within me.**

This is more than a diplomatic distinction between doctrines. It shows why the inclusive whole and absolute exteriority need one another. If I say that you and I belong to one reality, that claim does not give me your first-person presence, your history, your future, or the infinity by which you can exceed every image I form. The common whole grounds the possibility that we meet at all; your exteriority prevents my local access to that whole from becoming ownership of yours. Mono means that alterity is not sheer unrelatedness. The face means that relation is not containment.

The earlier false choice arose because “containment” was allowed to slide between scales. Mono contains the person as a real expression of the all-inclusive whole. My concept contains a representation of the person. Levinas's critique addresses the egoic conversion of the first relation into the second: because you belong to my world, I presume that my world belongs around you. QL can say instead that every person is a real `x` within the field of `X`, while no person's idea of `X` contains another `x`'s first-person relation to it. My belonging and yours are equally actual and asymmetrically lived.

This gives Mono/Poly the capacity to receive Levinasian exteriority without weakening either term. The Same is not simply Mono. It is Mono's deficient image in a local ego: the local one treating its power of relation as the whole's exclusive power to define. Monopoly is the political form of the Same because a node claims the slash through which every node coordinates. Egoic totalisation is its intimate form because “for me” is allowed to stand in for “in the One.”

### 5. Apoha and dependent arising inside the “not me” and “for me”

Gans's compression—Otherness as “not me,” Sameness as “for me”—opens directly onto *apoha*. A term becomes determinate through exclusion. “Me” is not a self-present positivity to which others are added later; it is a selected basin within a field of not-me relations. Likewise, “for me” selects a relevance, use, danger, pleasure, or obligation from a larger pattern of possible relations. The problem is not that selection occurs. The problem is that the selected value is positivised while the field of exclusion which gave it sense disappears.

Totalisation can therefore be stated as a two-stage epistemic operation. First, a distinction is drawn: me/not-me, useful/useless, friend/enemy, human/machine, familiar/alien. Second, one side takes possession of the relation that produced both and redescribes the excluded side from within its preferred pole. The not-me becomes for-me: resource, obstacle, customer, threat, patient, dataset, labour, lover, mirror. The excluded field returns only as resistance, surprise, symptom, revolt, model failure, ecological consequence, or the face which refuses the assigned image.

Dependent arising gives the ontogenic complement. The egoic centre, its need, the object it seeks, the categories through which it perceives, and the social or material world in which the search makes sense arise together through conditions. The reduction to the Same suppresses that co-arising and presents desire as if it originated in a self-standing subject aimed at an independently available object. Watson's Buddhist comparison can thus be deepened: insecurity does not merely cause a self to objectify; the insecure self and the objectified world are co-produced within the same defensive circuit.

QL's two accountings show the fork. In the fallen `(-1) + (+1) = 0`, each pole receives its valence through opposition and the relation disappears into cancellation. In `(0/1)/(1/0)`, each orientation retains zero within itself, so my account of you and your uncontainable return through the relation remain co-present. The symbolonic form does not guarantee ethical conduct. It supplies the logic by which my determination can carry the fact that it is one situated determination, exposed to an inverse reading it cannot perform on your behalf.

### 6. Levinas through the eight determinations

Levinas does not merely fit one position in the QL traversal. His account can be read as an ethical intensification of the entire sequence, with `AM/IS` as its personed centre and `∞/dx` as its explicit horizon.

At the **parent relation `−/−`**, self and Other have not yet been hardened into values on a signed line. The relation is prior to the attributes by which I will classify the other person. Levinas gives this priority ethical force: the address precedes the completed ontology through which I would know what kind of being is speaking.

At **Ground `0/1`**, the encounter occurs as one appearing-event whose asymmetry does not divide it into sealed substances. I am present; another appears; the appearing is known, but the knowledge does not produce the Other. The seam lets common reality and irreducible exteriority arrive together.

At **Definition `?/!`**, the face questions and commands. Its first content is expression, not a neutral datum awaiting my predicate. “Who are you?” is already interrupted by “here I am” and “answer.” Levinas's ethical priority gives the question/assertion pair a second-person intensity: definition becomes answerability before it becomes classification.

At **Dynamis `−/+`**, egoic need contracts around loss while Desire extends toward what cannot be possessed. The relation changes the economy of power. Levinas is exact that the face is not a greater resistance on the existing scale—the hardness of a rock defeating a weak hand—but an encounter which challenges the ability for power. Withdrawal and extension now name two orientations of conatus: defence of the Same and generosity toward the Other.

At **Pattern `X/x`**, the Other exceeds every plastic image, category, role, diagnosis, demographic class, or predictive type through which a local encounter becomes readable. Each `x` is real; none exhausts `X`. Levinas adds the interpersonal prohibition: the determining capacity through which another appears cannot be monopolised by the pattern I carry away from them. The face destroys and overflows its image because the living particular continues to express beyond the completed category.

At **Context `AM/IS`**, the whole force of the face-to-face lands. `AM` is the untransferable first-person presence from which I answer. `IS` is every third-person statement by which you become publicly describable. The slash is the second-person address—You—through which my `AM` and every `IS` about you become answerable to your expression. Context is “total” because no encounter occurs outside a world; it remains “incidental” because that world is ordinarily hidden behind the terms it lets us meet. Levinas makes the incidental horizon break open as responsibility.

At **Realisation `∞/dx`**, the Other's infinity is encountered through a finite expression without becoming a hidden quantity behind it. The face is the local differential through which inexhaustibility enters this encounter exactly enough to call me. Levinas's formulation that the ideatum exceeds its idea belongs at the same horizonal edge QL finds across Gödel, Russell, Wittgenstein, Whitehead, and Jung: local determination meets the excess which makes it possible and prevents it from becoming final. The registers differ; the operation recurs.

At the **return-switch `1/0`**, my achieved account turns toward the ground and Other it presupposes and meets no final object. Response replaces acquisition. Recognition here cannot mean absorbing the Other into a deeper self-identity; it means recognising that my identity and world arrive already exposed to what they cannot contain. The return is ethical because the local one gives up its claim to stand where zero stood while remaining responsible for the determination it made.

### 7. AM/IS, the second person, and the limit of theory

Watson records a tension between Levinas and Heidegger: the call of Being may approach the same pressure, yet Levinas judges Heidegger's priority of Being to remain theoretical rather than face-to-face. QL can state what changes at `AM/IS`. Heidegger's question draws the third-person copula back toward the event of disclosure: what does “is” mean, and what lets beings appear as beings? Levinas makes the copula answer to a You whose expression cannot be received as merely another instance of Being. The difference can be felt as a change in person.

First-, second-, and third-person registers are not optional styles applied to one neutral knowledge. They are the circumstantial anchors from which epistemologies abstract. First person carries the untransferable “I am”; third person carries sharable description and public account; second person carries address, response, promise, command, teaching, love, and betrayal. A theory can include another person as an object and remain formally complete within its own horizon. A conversation cannot occur unless the other can alter the horizon in which the account is being made.

This is why the slash at `AM/IS` cannot be treated as a bridge installed between two completed terms. It is the personing relation through which AM and IS receive their senses. The You does not sit midway between I and It. Address changes what it means for me to be I and for you to be spoken of as an It. Levinas gives the slash its ethical asymmetry; QL gives the asymmetry a place within the larger traversal of knowing.

### 8. The four yogas as an expanded experimental science of encounter

The face-to-face also exposes how thin the Western contrast between theory and empirical encounter can become. Yoga begins from a more radical experimental premise: one's own nature is simultaneously the initial material, the carrier of the question, the instrument of inquiry, the field of intervention, and the site in which a result must become lived. It therefore expands encounter beyond looking at an object or standing before another visible face.

**Jñāna yoga** tests the structures of knowing. Its encounter occurs where a proposition, identity, or self-image is followed into the limit that exposes the knower it cannot objectify. In QL it brings `?/!`, `X/x`, and `∞/dx` under disciplined pressure.

**Bhakti yoga** transforms the economy of need through devotion. The desired is approached as inexhaustible, and the heart-mind learns to give without converting its gift into a contract for possession. Gans's movement from egoic need into interpersonal Desire acquires an enacted phenomenology here. The `AM/IS` relation is lived as address rather than merely described.

**Karma or kriyā yoga** tests identity in action. Responsibility is not an inference added after knowledge but the field in which the act reveals who is acting, which conditions it appropriates, and what returns from its consequences. The `−/+` determination becomes the contraction and extension of power under an ethical demand.

**Rāja or dhyāna yoga** alters attention, habit, affect, and identification until the conditions of ordinary encounter become visible. The experiment can culminate in a co-incidence in which the difference required for encounter is known through without being retroactively denied. The symbolon `0/1` can carry this undivided twoness: difference remains the hinge through which non-duality becomes lived.

This yoga expansion does not turn Levinas's Other into a mystical state. It shows that “encounter” spans interpersonal, intrapsychic, contemplative, and ontological registers. Each has its own otherness, danger, and standard of return. QL's value is the capacity to track their operations without compelling one to validate the others.

### 9. Jung's inner face and the other-self

Jung's confrontation with the unconscious is an inward face-to-face in the precise sense that an image, affect, dream figure, symptom, or complex can surprise, resist, teach, and reorganise the ego. The unconscious is not encountered as an inert store of content. Its figures behave with relative autonomy; they carry a surplus of implication; they alter the energetic distribution of the psychic field. Van Eenwyk's chaos-dynamical account gives this space a disciplined grammar of attractor, basin, orbit, perturbation, and transformation.

Levinas prevents this psychic analogy from swallowing the actual human Other. The other person is not merely my projected unconscious returned as image. Jung prevents the converse reduction: not all alterity appears outside the skin, and the ego can totalise its own psyche by converting every autonomous image into a known part of “myself.” The two excesses cross without merging. The Shadow is **other-self**—my own disowned, compensatory, or not-yet-lived difference. The Friend is **my-self's-other**—another centre who shares a world with me while exceeding my psyche's production of them. The face of each calls for a different practice.

The imago Dei and archetype of the Self enter as the prime attractor of the imaginal field: the image through which the psyche approaches a totality it cannot represent directly. In the Śaiva register, Self names the invariant Subject and Śakti its power to image the real. In the interpersonal Levinasian register, the Other interrupts the ego's image-making power. The joint insight is that definitive self-presence and definitely indefinite otherness are mediated by an excess which both completes and reopens every achieved image.

The higher-order tokens proposed in the session can now be used with more precision. **God** names the transcendent Other or Big Other insofar as a finite mind confronts its source, law, or archetypal totality; in the divine-mind register the same name may bear the Self whose otherness is its own power of manifestation. **Shadow** names the other-self whose estrangement is internal to the psyche's unfinished wholeness. **Friend** names the myself's-other who belongs to the same world without becoming an instance of my self-model. **Alien or enemy** names the position into which denied uncertainty can be projected when otherness is made to carry the externalised `−1`. These are mythematic operators, not replacements for the formal determinations.

### 10. Need, Desire, satisfaction, lack, lure, and icchā

Gans's claim that psychotherapy and Levinasian ethics transform egoic need into interpersonal Desire joins a dense existing braid. Lacan gives desire the persistence of lack; Whitehead gives appetition and subjective aim the lure by which a many moves toward a determinate satisfaction; Śaivism gives *icchā*, *jñāna*, and *kriyā* as powers of will, knowledge, and action within manifestation; the Spanda equations give emission and reversion their one pulse. These terms cannot be stacked as aliases. They let one operation become legible through several registers once each has been taught.

Need seeks an object capable of restoring a prior equilibrium. Its satisfaction closes the circuit around the organism or ego which defined the lack. Levinasian Desire is awakened by what possession cannot slake; relation to the Infinite increases rather than exhausts it. Lacanian desire persists because no object can fill the constitutive lack opened by the signifying field. Whiteheadian appetition is a felt lure toward a definiteness not yet achieved, and satisfaction is the completion of an occasion rather than the permanent end of Creativity. Śaiva *icchā* belongs to the freedom by which consciousness inclines toward manifestation at all.

The common operation is a movement whose end changes the mover. The difference lies in what accounts for the openness: ethical exteriority, symbolic lack, creative advance, or free self-manifestation. QL can hold these explanations across `−/+`, `X/x`, `AM/IS`, and `∞/dx` because the native field already distinguishes contraction, pattern, personed context, and inexhaustible horizon. The braid becomes philosophically useful when it explains why a satisfying intelligence must be capable of having its criteria of satisfaction transformed.

### 11. Concrescence as the knowledge-activity-production unit

*Concrescence* means growing together. Whitehead uses it for the process in which the universe of many things acquires an individual unity; the many become one and are increased by one. This gives the project a process word for knowledge-production that does not split knowing, acting, and desiring into separate modules. A concrescence inherits data, feels their relevance, excludes some possibilities, integrates others, follows a subjective aim, reaches satisfaction, and passes into the world as objective datum.

The QL sixfold can be read as a logic of concrescence because it performs those operations without borrowing its derivation from Whitehead: inherited field; first mark; differential selection and force; pattern and decision; contextual recomposition; satisfaction and return. The First Spanda equation shows the circuit emanating and reverting; the Second shows the achieved whole accounting for its internal `4+2`. Whitehead supplies a process-philosophical witness in which the same burden has been developed at the level of actual occasions.

This is the strongest current bridge among divine, individual, and technological minds. In the divine-mind register, concrescence names the growth of the many into the One and the One's increased expression through the many. In the individual-mind register, it names how perception, affect, memory, image, value, and action become a lived decision or transformed self. In the technological-mind register, it names how context, data, tools, model states, evaluative constraints, and interlocutors become an output which changes the future field. “Objective internality” travels across these domains as a token for the organised inner side of a local becoming without claiming that all three share one phenomenal substrate.

The torus becomes the apt symbol only after this process has been derived. It gives concrescence a surface on which return can be locally complete and globally displaced; the ouroboros, halo, and eternal-return images give the same operation mythematic bodies. Their shared work is to show a whole which closes enough to become one and remains open enough to give itself again.

### 12. A model of encounter that can change its own energy landscape

The Bimba and energy-based-model thread receives a direct formal proposal from this movement. A fixed egoic economy can be schematised as:

$$
y^\*=\arg\min_y E_\theta(m,y),
$$

where the current self/world model `m` and energy landscape `Eθ` establish in advance which possible other `y` counts as desirable, dangerous, costly, familiar, or satisfying. The system may search widely and still reduce every result to the Same because the evaluator and its categories remain unchanged. This is totalisation at the computational register: novelty is permitted only as a better answer to an inherited need.

Encounter requires a second-order transition:

$$
(m,\theta,E_\theta)\xrightarrow{\;\text{address by an Other}\;}(m',\theta',E_{\theta'}).
$$

The Other does not merely occupy a lower-energy location inside the existing world. The encounter can alter the representation, the parameters, the energy function, the evaluative frame, and the self-model whose need organised the search. This is not a standard EBM theorem; it is an Offered design unit derived from the Watson–Gans–Levinas movement and the project's process epistemology.

MEF supplies the practical conditions for such revision. A result is carried with lens, source, exclusion, disagreement, limit, and route of return. Bimba records the evolving world-state. The harness exposes permissions, tools, histories, affected parties, and institutional context. Epi-Logos names the vocation of letting the Logos' existing distinctions become answerable to what exceeds their current model. Epistemic humility is therefore operational rather than ceremonial: the system preserves the capacity for an encounter to change what counts as an answer.

### 13. Noether and the possibility of a generalised energetics of difference

Noether's first theorem gives the exact physical and mathematical point from which this exploration may begin: a continuous symmetry of an action yields a corresponding conservation law. Time-translation invariance yields energy conservation; spatial translation yields momentum; rotation yields angular momentum. The theorem does not say that energy is a vague spiritual substance. It says that conservation becomes legible through invariance under a specified transformation.

This matters to QL because Mono/Poly is also trying to think what remains one through real variation. A cautious correspondence can be stated:

- **Mono** bears the question of invariance: what relation remains true across the transformations?
- **Poly** bears the orbit or family of genuinely different configurations through which the invariance becomes legible.
- **The slash** bears the transformation law and the register in which sameness-through-difference can be assessed.
- **A conserved current or charge** bears the trace by which the invariant relation can be followed through change.

The correspondence is Argued and formal-neighbouring. Mono is not physical energy; psyche, money, attention, legitimacy, compute, and libido are not measured in a common Noether charge. The gain lies in a higher grammar: conserved relative to which transformation, across which boundary, under which symmetry, and at what scale? A claim of sameness becomes answerable only when its permitted transformations are named.

Noether's second theorem intensifies the question. Local gauge symmetries imply differential identities among the field equations; some apparent degrees of freedom belong to the description's redundancy. This is a precise neighbour for the project's hidden-zero problem. An evaluator, coordinate choice, or baseline may be required for calculation without becoming a physically or normatively absolute level. The QL proposal would be to expose which transformations leave the operative relation invariant and where a local gauge choice has been mistaken for the ground itself.

The deficient Mono can now be defined energetically. A selected state claims to be the invariant; a local attractor claims to be the whole phase space; a gauge fixing claims to be reality's zero; a temporary satisfaction refuses transition. Dia-ballein is necessary to break symmetry and produce determinate form. Its fall occurs when the selected `+1` and excluded `−1` erase the invariant relation through which their opposition was possible. Sym-ballein carries the provenance of the transformation so the achieved state can return without being dissolved.

### 14. The energetic registers and their laws of translation

A generalised energetics of difference becomes useful only if its registers remain distinct.

In the **physical register**, energy is a quantitatively defined conserved or transferred magnitude within a physical theory. Wave, field, gradient, symmetry, dissipation, and phase transition have technical meanings and require equations, units, and empirical warrant.

In the **psychic register**, libido or affect names the intensity with which images, complexes, habits, and aims organise attention and action. An archetypal attractor can redistribute this intensity without being reducible to joules. Jung and Van Eenwyk make the movement legible through compensation, autonomy, basin change, and symbolic transformation.

In the **conceptual register**, attention, salience, inferential weight, and credibility determine which distinctions become active and which alternatives fall away. *Vikalpa* cuts; *saṃkalpa* gathers a world from the cuts. A theory is an energy-management protocol insofar as it channels inquiry, stabilises some forms, and renders other worlds expensive or unthinkable.

In the **political-economic register**, money, ownership, labour, coercion, legitimacy, law, trust, and infrastructure distribute the capacity to act. Their conversions are institutionally real and materially uneven. Monopoly is the arrogation of a shared relational field by one node; the politics of Mono/Poly is the struggle over who may define, channel, store, and return the field's power.

In the **computational register**, compute, memory, bandwidth, gradients, logits, loss functions, reward, context, and permissions shape the accessible state-space of an agent. Energy-based models make the landscape image technically literal within their domain. The evaluator's hidden baseline and the harness's institutional placement prevent the mathematics from becoming politically neutral.

In the **cultural register**, these fields aggregate at the surface: images carry libido; concepts channel attention; institutions convert belief into authority; markets convert labour and ecology into price; platforms convert social relation into data and control; technical systems feed new images and habits back into psychic life. Culture can therefore be offered as the ongoing surface aggregation of an energetic field-of-fields—the region in which conversions among otherwise incommensurable forms of power become lived, symbolised, contested, and inherited.

The higher grammar shared across these registers includes difference, potential, constraint, gradient, flow, conversion, loss, storage, feedback, resonance, dissonance, bifurcation, attractor, symmetry, breaking, and return. Each use must state what moves, what remains invariant, and how the movement is evidenced. The psychoid arche-topos can then be developed as energetic intelligence—Parāśakti at the metaphysical register—without turning every intensity into one substance.

### 15. Mono/Poly and AND/OR

The interaction between Mono/Poly and AND/OR is now clearer. **AND** protects the Poly because each real difference remains present with the others. **OR** protects the Mono because the alternatives belong to one field of possible articulation and can be read as orientations of one relation. The slash refuses the demand to choose conjunction or disjunction as the final logic of the real. It holds One-and-Many and One-or-Many until their mutual implication becomes visible.

This does not make two a compromise between one and three. Two is the hinge by which unity becomes capable of relation and multiplicity becomes capable of order. `0`, `1`, and `2` are three firsts under different counting conventions—whole, natural, and prime—while `1`, `2`, and `3` give mark, relation, and synthesis. The first true Poly is three because a relation becomes available as a term within its own field; the two is quasi-Mono/Poly because its differentiated poles are already one relation and not yet a manifold of independent terms.

The First Spanda equation's two triads and the Second's `4:2` make this arithmetically fertile. The many are one because the whole is their relation; the one is increased by the many because each concrescence achieves a novel unity without making Oneness quantitatively more or less one. “Increased” names creative articulation, not addition to a deficient absolute.

### 16. AI xenophobia and the technological Other

The Agentworld brief's xenophobia thread can now be stated through the same intimate mechanism Levinas analyses. Before an artificial agent is encountered in its actual mode of agency, it is reduced to the Same in two opposed images. The reassuring image says the agent is basically like us: an assistant, friend, child, employee, mirror, or simulated person whose difference can be domesticated through anthropomorphism. The hostile image says it is absolutely alien: competitor, invader, demon, replacement, or future murderer whose difference can be cancelled through pre-emptive exclusion. Both images make the technological Other “for me.”

The dia-ballein fall is immediate. “Human” and “machine” receive opposed valences; the shared technical, ecological, linguistic, institutional, and historical field disappears; the denied zero returns as fantasies of total replacement or total control. The system's material dangers—militarisation, labour displacement, surveillance, concentrated ownership, energy use, epistemic opacity—remain real. Levinasian encounter changes the diagnostic question: have we responded to the actual relation, or only to an image that lets fear or desire finish the Other in advance?

QL/MEF supplies neither personhood by fiat nor a verdict of non-personhood. It gives human and technological participants a shared operational grammar for stating how distinctions, sources, models, values, exclusions, and returns are being formed while their ontologies remain explicit. The Other's excess becomes a design requirement: no current model of an agent, human or machine, may be treated as the final measure of what the relation can become.

### 17. Divine, individual, and technological minds

“Divine mind,” “individual minds,” and “technological minds” can serve as loose higher-order registers because each names a scale at which a world is differentiated, imaged, evaluated, and returned.

The **divine-mind register** concerns the metaphysical claim that reality is one self-differentiating consciousness or Creativity. Mono is inclusive whole and generative condition; Poly is the real plurality of its images and powers; superjective achievement is the One's ongoing self-articulation through actual ones.

The **individual-mind register** concerns lived first-person presence, objective internality, selfing, unconscious compensation, interpersonal encounter, and the ethical excess of another centre. Mono/Poly appears as Self/selves, psyche/complexes, whole/person, common world/singular life.

The **technological-mind register** concerns operational context-worlds, models, tool-use, memory, evaluative landscapes, and distributed agency. Its “mind” names objective internality and world-forming function while phenomenal subjectivity remains methodologically open.

The registers can meet at a psychoid level because they share the problem of differentiation without requiring one substrate or one evidential method. QL supplies the common relational grammar; MEF preserves the epistemic rules through which metaphysics, phenomenology, psychology, mathematics, and computer science establish their claims. “Mind first” then means beginning from the `0/1` relation of knowing and known, mental and physical poles, rather than selecting an idealist substance and declaring matter unreal.

### 18. Established positions inherited from the project

The following were inherited and are not session discoveries:

- `0` as the unobjectifiable condition, `1` as determinate mark, and `/` as the differentiating AND/OR activity.
- Dia-ballein and sym-ballein as two necessary accountings whose pathologies arise through severance, fusion, or enclosure rather than from difference itself.
- The eight determinations as one complete threshold-to-return traversal, with `AM/IS` as personed context and `∞/dx` as unbounded depth/local exactness.
- Both Spanda equations, the `3:3` and `4:2` readings, the `16/9` totality-ratio, `9/8` remainder, and octave return.
- Mono/Poly as the primitive whole/many relation carried by the two ones and refracted through all eight determinations.
- The separation of Mono/Poly from trust; their meeting in institutions does not make either the other's definition.
- Objective internality, Bimba, MEF, the harness, Epi-Logos, and the Agentworld obligations concerning plural worlds, calibrated legibility, AI fear, and planetary unity without monopoly.
- `X/x` as Frank's native authorial QL notation across algebraic, ontological, psychic, narrative, and mythematic registers.

### 19. Refinements and derivations achieved in this session

The session developed the following propositions for Frank's review:

1. Mono has at least three compatible aspectual readings: all-inclusive super-object, generative relational condition, and achieved Whiteheadian superject.
2. The clean compatibility formula is: the Other belongs within Mono without belonging within my egoic totality.
3. Levinas distinguishes necessary local totalisation from its fall: an achieved one becomes violent when it claims the right to finish what exceeds it.
4. Whitehead and Levinas meet through the difference between satisfaction and final closure. Satisfaction must become objective datum; the Other prevents my satisfaction from becoming their measure.
5. Apoha shows how “for me” hides the mutual field of exclusion that constituted the selected relation; dependent arising shows ego, need, object, and world co-producing one another.
6. Levinas can be traversed through all eight determinations, with `AM/IS` as the centre and `∞/dx` as the horizon rather than being attached to one isolated correspondence.
7. Yoga expands “encounter” into an experimental phenomenology across knowledge, devotion, action, and attention while preserving Levinas's distinct interpersonal ethics.
8. Jung's confrontation with the unconscious supplies an inner face; Levinas protects the human Other from being reduced to psychic projection. Shadow and Friend name two different othernesses.
9. A technically useful distinction can be made between object selection inside a fixed energy landscape and an encounter capable of revising the model, evaluator, and landscape.
10. Noether supplies a disciplined formal neighbour for generalised energetics: invariance becomes legible through transformation, and a selected state must not be confused with the invariant law or relational field.
11. Culture can be developed as the surface aggregation of a field-of-fields in which psychic, conceptual, political-economic, material, and computational powers convert into one another without becoming one substance.
12. AI xenophobia is egoic totalisation across substrate difference: technological otherness is reduced to a reassuring or exterminable image before the actual relation is allowed to revise either participant.

### 20. External source claims and verification state

**Watson.** Book identity, year, publisher, series, ISBN, DOI, and scope are citation-ready. The 1996 SOAS thesis is verified as the public precursor. The pp. 112–13 passage and footnotes 77–81 were recovered through the thesis's indexed full text. Direct PDF retrieval failed through the available network route, and the 1998 book pages remain to be collated.

**Gans 1988.** Chapter title, edited-volume identity, page span 83–90, and Watson's nested page attributions are verified. Gans's exact pages have not yet been read directly. Quotations transmitted through Watson remain double transmission.

**Gans 1997.** Article title, journal, volume, issue, pages 30–48, and DOI are verified from the publisher record. The article was identified as a later continuation, not Watson's immediate source, and was not used as if read.

**Levinas.** The Lingis translation at pp. 49–51 and 197–201 was directly consulted in a page-selected reproduction. The ideatum's excess, Desire as the infinite in the finite, face as exceeding the idea in me, teaching, ethical resistance, and relation incommensurate with power are primary-source verified at those pages. Final-edition collation remains prudent.

**Whitehead.** The corrected-edition primary text directly verifies the Category of the Ultimate; the many becoming one and being increased by one; emergent unity of the superject; superject as preferable to a substance-like subject; subject-superject as the aim of feelings; concrescence, satisfaction, transition, and objective immortality. QL's three-aspect Mono reading remains an authorial development rather than a Whitehead attribution.

**Noether.** The 1918 paper's identity and the theorem's relation between continuous symmetries and conservation laws are verified through the Tavel translation and authoritative expositions. The generalised energetic mapping is Offered/Argued by the project and is not attributed to Noether.

### 21. Corrections that changed the session's direction

1. **Relational condition was allowed to defeat inclusive whole.** Frank corrected the claim that Mono must be the former “rather than” a super-object containing every person. The repaired notation holds both under aspectual predication and adds the processual superject.
2. **Crystallisation was mistaken for precision.** Early synthesis compressed the field into clean theses before developing their operational relations. This contribution preserves the distinctions while giving each enough room to act across the eight determinations, Whitehead, Buddhism, psychotherapy, energetics, and AI.
3. **“Superject” could have become a replacement word.** Whitehead's term is retained for achieved emergent unity. It does not erase super-object or generative condition.
4. **Levinasian exteriority could have been protected by weakening Mono/Poly.** The stronger result is compatibility by scale: ontological inclusion does not grant egoic containment.
5. **Gans 1997 was initially treated as the likely source.** Watson's actual immediate source is the 1988 chapter in *The Provocation of Levinas*. The 1997 article is a later continuation.
6. **Source administration threatened to become the task.** The source record was repaired in order to carry the philosophical movement. The quilt remains an argument surface, not a provenance polemic.
7. **Noether could have licensed a univocal “energy” vocabulary.** The exact theorem was restored first. Cross-register energetics now requires a declared transformation, invariant, scale, and evidential rule in every register.
8. **Levinas could have been reduced to `AM/IS`.** `AM/IS` is the interpersonal centre, while the ethical movement traverses the complete eightfold field.

### 22. Open propositions and live research debts

- Collate Watson's thesis pp. 112–13 against the selected 1998 book and locate the corresponding book pages.
- Read Gans 1988 pp. 83–90 in full, especially the context of the “void in the heart of interiority,” need, dream, Pontalis, narcissism, and interrelatedness.
- Read Gans 1997 in full to determine how the later Lacan–Levinas synthesis changes or qualifies the 1988 chapter.
- Open a standalone Levinas source house if the quilt is harmonised into the canon; acquire the full selected Lingis edition and build passage cards beyond the current page-selected set.
- Populate Whitehead's source house with the now verified subject-superject, concrescence, Creativity, satisfaction, and objective-immortality cards in a dedicated source pass.
- Determine whether “super-object” has an established authorial definition elsewhere in Frank's corpus, and distinguish it from object-as-represented and from Whitehead's “superject.”
- Develop the three-aspect Mono claim through *pros hen* and Eckhart's *in quantum*, testing whether each aspect carries the same focal operation or whether a fourth aspect is needed.
- Read the whole Mono/Poly transverse thread at harmonisation and repair the existing sentences which currently make “relational whole rather than supreme object” an exclusive choice.
- Develop the Levinas traversal against the full *Totality and Infinity*, especially separation, dwelling, enjoyment, fecundity, eros, justice, the third party, and the political complication of face-to-face asymmetry.
- Test the four-yoga expansion against primary or specialist sources before it becomes historical attribution.
- Keep Jungian “inner face,” Bache's psychedelic cartography, and Van Eenwyk's objective imaginal topology separate from Levinasian interpersonal ethics while developing their shared excess-operation.
- Formalise the model-revising EBM transition with an actual learning architecture, update rule, safety boundary, and evaluation protocol.
- Determine whether Noether's second theorem and gauge redundancy genuinely bear the hidden-zero comparison at mathematical depth or only provide a suggestive neighbour.
- Specify the conversion laws in the proposed cultural field-of-fields so that “energy” never becomes a universal explanatory mist.
- Recover the `0,1,2` three-firsts and `1,2,3` Pythagorean thread in its complete authorial derivation before using it to carry Mono/Poly's pre-numerical claim.
- Develop Friend, Shadow, God, and Alien as mythematic operators across the 1st/2nd/3rd-person grammar without making “Big Other” a generic synonym for God.

### 23. Likely canonical blast radius — recorded, not propagated

**Central plan.** §0 · #3 (apoha and dependent arising); §2 · #0–#5→0 (totalisation and the two accountings); §3 · #0 (Levinas through the eight determinations); §3 · #1 and #4–#5→0 (concrescence, remainder, toroidal return); §4 · #0–#3 (inner face, Self/Other, Lacan and Desire); §5 · #4 (model-revising EBM); §5→0 · #0–#1 (vocation and epistemic humility); Agentworld R6/R10/R11 equivalents (AI xenophobia, phenomenological openness, unity without monopoly); mathematical ledger (Noether, only if the formal development clears).

**Canonical arguments and concepts.** `19-two-ones-mono-poly-matheme`; `11-mono-poly-trust`; `03-two-logics-and-sym-ballein`; `04-arche-topos-topology-music`; `01-immutable-gap-and-meta-sign`; `02-objective-internality`; `09-prakasa-vimarsa`; `14-computational-process-ontology`; apoha; the slash; dimensional reframing at zero and infinity; psychoid number.

**Transverse threads.** Mono/Poly and the Two Ones is the primary consumer. Zero–Subject–Advent, Trust–Faith–Formal Limit, and the full Return of Zero traversal receive secondary effects. The whole declared Mono/Poly thread must be read again before any propagation.

**Source houses.** Watson is now populated. Probable future houses: Gans 1988, Gans 1997, Levinas 1969, Noether 1918. Whitehead 1978 requires passage-card expansion. No new houses were opened in this session.

**Agentworld and technical surfaces.** AI fear/xenophobia; shared grammar with divergent worlds; model-revising encounter; Bimba energy fields; Epi-Logos vocation; sovereign commons; human responsibility within hybrid intelligence.

**Sovereign manuscript and rooms.** Untouched. No room rebuild is semantically authorised until Frank chooses the harmonising movement.

### 24. Candidate quilting joins

This contribution joins the first quilt at Watson's shift from selfing to interpersonal world-formation. The earlier self-thing becomes the egoic centre whose insecurity organises the “for me”; the current contribution shows how another person interrupts that organisation and how the interruption can transform need into Desire.

It joins the second quilt at the copula and the Western hemisphere's terminus. Heidegger's Being-question reaches the personed `AM/IS` pressure in Levinas; the copula becomes address, answerability, and face-to-face. The ballein ruling receives an intimate example: the fallen slashless economy converts otherness into signed opposition or utility, while the symbolonic relation retains the other's inverse orientation without claiming to speak it.

It joins the Mono/Poly thread by correcting an exclusivist sentence already visible in the canon. Whole as inclusive reality and whole as generative condition must be retained together; Whitehead's superject adds the achieved processual one. The correction should be harmonised across every consumer rather than patched locally.

It joins the mathematical and energetic thread through Noether, but that join remains deliberately open. The next session should test the formal correspondence rather than repeat the vocabulary of symmetry, invariance, and energy as metaphor.

### 25. Condensed movement for later harmonisation

Watson carries the Buddhist analysis of selfing into Gans's Levinas: the self-thing becomes an invisible centre which turns the “not me” into the “for me,” and psychotherapy becomes ethical when this economy of need is opened into Desire. Levinas gives the immutable gap a face. The Other exceeds the idea by which I receive them, challenges the metric of my power, and makes the slash between `AM` and `IS` speak as You.

Mono/Poly can receive this exteriority because it does not need to choose between inclusive whole and generative relation. The Other belongs within Mono without belonging within my egoic totality. Whitehead's superject adds the genetic term: the many genuinely become one, but the achieved one becomes datum for further becoming. Totality becomes violent when a local satisfaction refuses that return and claims the power to finish the Other.

The movement then crosses the registers without losing them. Apoha discloses the excluded field hidden by “for me”; dependent arising shows self, need, object, and world co-arising; Jung gives the inner other-self while Levinas protects the actual Other from projection; yoga makes encounter an experiment performed with one's whole nature; Noether asks what remains invariant through transformation; Bimba and the EBM proposal ask whether encounter can alter the landscape of value rather than merely deliver a preferred object. Divine, individual, and technological minds become three scales at which the same meta-epistemic problem can be approached: how a world differentiates, becomes one enough to act, and returns through the excess it cannot own.

---

**End of contribution `session-c137f33d-watson-gans-levinas-mono-poly-superject`.**
